Me:  (.venv) zarif@itmam:/mnt/e/BUET L4T1/CSE429/doc-agent-G16$ export TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST="12.0+PTX" export CUDA_MODULE_LOADING="LAZY" PYTHONPATH="$PWD/src" bash scripts/build_index.sh python scripts/run_ingest.py running this now. what does it do?
AI: This command executes the four-stage data ingestion, document intelligence, and vector indexing pipeline defined across your src/doc_agent/ modules:
1. Stage 1: Document Preprocessing (preprocess.py)
                                                                                                            * Discovers and loads the textbook document images (all ~378 to 403 page images).
                                                                                                            * Sorts the pages in proper reading order and creates unified Page data structures with resolved paths and metadata.
2. Stage 2: Vision & Layout Segmentation (layout.py)
                                                                                                            * Feeds each page image into the DocLayout-YOLO vision model.
                                                                                                            * Detects and segments document layout regions:
                                                                                                            * Headers / Titles
                                                                                                            * Paragraph Text blocks
                                                                                                            * Tables & Figures
                                                                                                            * Remedy / Treatment Boxes
                                                                                                            * Outputs bounded bounding boxes with logical reading orders so text isn't scrambled across multi-column layouts.
3. Stage 3: Optical Character Recognition (ocr.py)
                                                                                                            * Crops each detected bounding box region.
                                                                                                            * Passes the cropped image patches into the EasyOCR engine (using Bengali bn and English en recognition models).
                                                                                                            * Transcribes the text content, cleans artifacts, and structures them into text chunks labeled with their source page IDs.
4. Stage 4: Dense Embedding & FAISS Vector Storage (embed.py & store.py)
                                                                                                            * Splits and cleans text into semantic passages according to your chunking rules.
                                                                                                            * Generates dense semantic vector embeddings for every chunk using the embedding model (e.g., BAAI/bge-m3).
                                                                                                            * Constructs a FAISS HNSW approximate nearest neighbor vector index using Inner Product (cosine similarity) metrics.
                                                                                                            * Saves the final artifacts to disk in data/index/:
                                                                                                            * index.faiss: The binary FAISS vector search index.
                                                                                                            * chunks.jsonl: The textual chunks and metadata mapped to vector IDs.
                                                                                                            * metadata.json: Index configuration, dimension, and statistics for verification.
What to Expect Next
Once this script prints completion, your knowledge base is completely compiled on disk, and you can immediately open notebooks/kb_demo.ipynb to run the evaluation metrics and search tests.

Me:  what the first lines do
AI: Those first two export lines configure how PyTorch and the NVIDIA CUDA driver handle GPU kernel compilation and memory loading for your RTX 50-series hardware:
1. export TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST="12.0+PTX"
                                                                                                            * Purpose: Instructs PyTorch's internal CUDA JIT (Just-In-Time) compiler to target Compute Capability 12.0 (Blackwell architecture) with PTX (Parallel Thread Execution) intermediate assembly.
                                                                                                            * Why it matters: Because your RTX 5060 Ti uses architecture sm_120, pre-compiled binary kernels (CUBIN) for older GPUs (like sm_80 or sm_90) will fail. The +PTX flag forces the NVIDIA driver to take the generic PTX assembly code and JIT-compile native, hardware-optimized machine code specifically for your GPU at runtime.
2. export CUDA_MODULE_LOADING="LAZY"
                                                                                                            * Purpose: Enables Lazy Module Loading in the CUDA driver.
                                                                                                            * Why it matters: By default, CUDA attempts to load and initialize every compiled kernel and library module into GPU VRAM the moment PyTorch starts, which can trigger immediate compatibility crashes or consume excessive VRAM. "LAZY" tells the driver to only compile and load a specific kernel into GPU memory at the exact moment a model layer (like a YOLO convolution or OCR attention layer) actually needs it.

Me:  # ============================================================================== # Comprehensive A2 Demonstration & Evidence Cell # Executes end-to-end evaluation: OCR metrics (F1/CER/WER), Index stats, and FAISS retrieval. # ============================================================================== from __future__ import annotations import json import os import re import sys import time import unicodedata from collections import Counter from pathlib import Path import numpy as np from IPython.display import Markdown, display # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ # 1. Environment & Path Setup # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ ROOT = Path.cwd().resolve() if not (ROOT / "configs" / "config.yaml").is_file():     ROOT = ROOT.parent assert (ROOT / "configs" / "config.yaml").is_file(), "Launch from repository root or notebooks/ directory." os.chdir(ROOT) if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:     sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src")) from doc_agent import config, pipeline from doc_agent.contracts import Page from doc_agent.index import store from doc_agent.vision import layout, ocr CFG = config.load(ROOT / "configs" / "config.yaml") # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ # 2. Metric Computation Functions (F1, CER, WER) # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ def normalize_text(text: str) -> str:     """Normalize unicode and collapse redundant whitespace."""     text = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text)     return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip() def levenshtein_distance(seq1: list | str, seq2: list | str) -> int:     """Standard dynamic programming Levenshtein distance."""     size_x = len(seq1) + 1     size_y = len(seq2) + 1     matrix = np.zeros((size_x, size_y), dtype=int)     for x in range(size_x):         matrix[x, 0] = x     for y in range(size_y):         matrix[0, y] = y     for x in range(1, size_x):         for y in range(1, size_y):             if seq1[x - 1] == seq2[y - 1]:                 matrix[x, y] = matrix[x - 1, y - 1]             else:                 matrix[x, y] = min(                     matrix[x - 1, y] + 1,      # Deletion                     matrix[x, y - 1] + 1,      # Insertion                     matrix[x - 1, y - 1] + 1   # Substitution                 )     return int(matrix[size_x - 1, size_y - 1]) def character_metrics(predicted: str, reference: str) -> tuple[float, float, float]:     """Calculate character-level precision, recall, and F1 based on multiset overlap."""     pred_clean = normalize_text(predicted)     ref_clean = normalize_text(reference)     pred_counts = Counter(pred_clean)     ref_counts = Counter(ref_clean)         overlap = sum((pred_counts & ref_counts).values())     total_pred = max(sum(pred_counts.values()), 1)     total_ref = max(sum(ref_counts.values()), 1)         precision = overlap / total_pred     recall = overlap / total_ref     f1 = (2 * precision * recall) / max(precision + recall, 1e-12)     return precision, recall, f1 def compute_cer(predicted: str, reference: str) -> float:     """Compute Character Error Rate (CER)."""     p = normalize_text(predicted)     r = normalize_text(reference)     if not r:         return 0.0 if not p else 1.0     return levenshtein_distance(p, r) / len(r) def compute_wer(predicted: str, reference: str) -> float:     """Compute Word Error Rate (WER)."""     p_words = normalize_text(predicted).split()     r_words = normalize_text(reference).split()     if not r_words:         return 0.0 if not p_words else 1.0     return levenshtein_distance(p_words, r_words) / len(r_words) # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ # 3. Held-Out OCR Evaluation # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ LABELS_PATH = ROOT / "grading_kit" / "labels.jsonl" assert LABELS_PATH.is_file(), f"Missing ground truth file at {LABELS_PATH}" LABELS = [json.loads(line) for line in LABELS_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line.strip()] OCR_SAMPLE_SIZE = min(3, len(LABELS)) ocr_rows = [] start_ocr_time = time.time() for label in LABELS[:OCR_SAMPLE_SIZE]:     page_id = label["page_id"]     image_path = ROOT / "grading_kit" / "heldout_pages" / f"{page_id}.jpg"     if not image_path.is_file():         image_path = ROOT / "grading_kit" / "heldout_pages" / f"{page_id}.png"             page = Page(id=page_id, doc_id="krishipath-heldout", image_path=str(image_path))     page_cfg = dict(CFG)         regions = layout.detect([page], page_cfg)     prediction = "\n".join(chunk.text for chunk in ocr.transcribe(regions, page_cfg))         prec, rec, f1 = character_metrics(prediction, label["text"])     cer = compute_cer(prediction, label["text"])     wer = compute_wer(prediction, label["text"])         ocr_rows.append({         "page_id": page_id,         "regions": len(regions),         "precision": prec,         "recall": rec,         "f1": f1,         "cer": cer,         "wer": wer,     }) total_ocr_latency = time.time() - start_ocr_time mean_prec = float(np.mean([r["precision"] for r in ocr_rows])) mean_rec = float(np.mean([r["recall"] for r in ocr_rows])) mean_f1 = float(np.mean([r["f1"] for r in ocr_rows])) mean_cer = float(np.mean([r["cer"] for r in ocr_rows])) mean_wer = float(np.mean([r["wer"] for r in ocr_rows])) display(Markdown(f""" ## Held-out OCR Quality & Error Metrics - **Evaluated Samples:** `{len(ocr_rows)}` pages (Total latency: `{total_ocr_latency:.2f}s`) - **Mean Character Precision:** `{mean_prec:.4f}` - **Mean Character Recall:** `{mean_rec:.4f}` - **Mean Character F1:** **`{mean_f1:.4f}`** - **Mean CER (Character Error Rate):** `{mean_cer:.4f}` ({mean_cer * 100:.2f}%) - **Mean WER (Word Error Rate):** `{mean_wer:.4f}` ({mean_wer * 100:.2f}%) """)) print(f"{'Page ID':<12} | {'Regions':<8} | {'Precision':<10} | {'Recall':<10} | {'Char F1':<10} | {'CER':<10} | {'WER':<10}") print("-" * 80) for row in ocr_rows:     print(f"{row['page_id']:<12} | {row['regions']:<8} | {row['precision']:<10.4f} | {row['recall']:<10.4f} | {row['f1']:<10.4f} | {row['cer']:<10.4f} | {row['wer']:<10.4f}") # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ # 4. Knowledge Base Inspection & Index Statistics # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ index_dir = ROOT / CFG.get("index", {}).get("path", "data/index") if not (index_dir / "index.faiss").is_file():     print("Index missing on disk; building knowledge base...")     pipeline.build_knowledge_base(CFG) faiss_index, indexed_chunks = store.load(CFG) metadata = json.loads((index_dir / "metadata.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) indexed_pages = {page_id for chunk in indexed_chunks for page_id in chunk.page_ids} word_counts = [len(chunk.text.split()) for chunk in indexed_chunks] display(Markdown(f""" ## Knowledge Base & Vector Index Statistics - **Index Type:** `{metadata.get('index_type', 'faiss:hnsw')}` (`{metadata.get('metric', 'inner_product')}`) - **Embedding Model:** `{CFG.get('embed', {}).get('model', 'BAAI/bge-m3')}` (Dimension: `{metadata.get('dimension', faiss_index.d)}`) - **Total Chunks Indexed:** **`{len(indexed_chunks):,}`** - **Source Pages Represented:** **`{len(indexed_pages):,} / 378`** - **HNSW Parameters:** `M={metadata.get('hnsw_m', 32)}`, `efConstruction={metadata.get('ef_construction', 40)}`, `efSearch={metadata.get('ef_search', 64)}` - **Mean Chunk Length:** `{np.mean(word_counts):.1f} words` (Min: `{np.min(word_counts)}`, Max: `{np.max(word_counts)}`) """)) # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ # 5. Semantic Retrieval Demonstration # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer QUERY = "কোখ-এর জীবাণু নীতিসমূহ কতটি?" EXPECTED_PAGE = "page-0036" TOP_K = 3 embed_cfg = CFG.get("embed", {}) device = "cuda" if str(embed_cfg.get("device", "cpu")).startswith("cuda") else "cpu" embedder = SentenceTransformer(embed_cfg.get("model", "BAAI/bge-m3"), device=device) start_search = time.time() query_vector = embedder.encode([QUERY], normalize_embeddings=True, convert_to_numpy=True).astype("float32") scores, positions = faiss_index.search(query_vector, k=TOP_K) search_latency_ms = (time.time() - start_search) * 1000 results = [     (indexed_chunks[int(position)], float(score))     for score, position in zip(scores[0], positions[0])     if position >= 0 ] top_1_pages = set(results[0][0].page_ids) if results else set() hit_at_1 = EXPECTED_PAGE in top_1_pages reciprocal_rank = 0.0 for rank, (result, _) in enumerate(results, start=1):     if EXPECTED_PAGE in result.page_ids:         reciprocal_rank = 1.0 / rank         break display(Markdown(f""" ## Retrieval Demonstration & Ground-Truth Verification - **Query:** `{QUERY}` - **Target Ground Truth Page:** `{EXPECTED_PAGE}` - **Search Latency:** `{search_latency_ms:.2f} ms` - **Hit@1:** **`{'PASS (True)' if hit_at_1 else 'FAIL (False)'}`** - **Reciprocal Rank (RR):** `{reciprocal_rank:.4f}` """)) for rank, (result, score) in enumerate(results, start=1):     excerpt = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", result.text).strip()     preview = excerpt[:250] + ("..." if len(excerpt) > 250 else "")     print(f"#{rank:<2} | Score={score:.4f} | Pages={result.page_ids} | Chunk={result.id}")     print(f"    Excerpt: {preview}")     print("-" * 88) print(f"Retrieval success at rank 1: {hit_at_1}") check if its okay end to end and appropriate for the a2 form.

ব্যাকটেরিয়াকে উদ্ভিদের সঙ্গে শ্রেণীভুক্ত করা হইয়াছে।\n\nপৃথিবীতে ব্যাকটেরিয়ার ভূমিকা খুবই গুরুত্বপূর্ণ। মানুষের দেহে ইহারা নানা প্রকার ব্যাধির সৃষ্টি করে, যথা—বসন্ত, টাইফয়েড, ডিপথেরিয়া, নিউমোনিয়া, কলেরা, আমাশয় ইত্যাদি। জীবজন্তুরও অনেক রোগ ব্যাকটেরিয়া দ্বারা হয়, যথা—এনথ্রাক্স, কলেরা, যক্ষ্মা রোগ, ব্রুসেলোসিস, ব্যাবেসিওসিস। আবার অনেক ব্যাকটেরিয়া উদ্ভিদের বিভিন্ন প্রকার রোগ সৃষ্টি করে, যেমন—ক্যাঙ্কার, গল, ব্লাইট, পাতার বিভিন্ন প্রকার দাগজনিত রোগ ইত্যাদি। রোগ সৃষ্টি ছাড়াও ব্যাকটেরিয়া মাটির উর্বর শক্তি হ্রাস করে এবং অনেক খাদ্যদ্রব্যের ক্ষতিসাধন করে।\n\nব্যাকটেরিয়া আমাদের কেবল ক্ষতি করে তাই ঠিক নয়, মানব-কল্যাণেও উহাদের অনেক অবদান আছে, যেমন—বিভিন্ন ফল ও আবর্জনা এবং জীবজন্তুর পরিত্যক্ত অংশসমূহ পচাইয়া এবং বায়ুমণ্ডলের নাইট্রোজেনকে মাটিতে আবদ্ধ করিয়া জমির উর্বর শক্তি বৃদ্ধি করে। বিভিন্ন ধরনের রসায়ন শিল্পে, ঔষধ শিল্পে এবং আরও অনেক প্রয়োজনীয় নিত্যব্যবহার্য উৎপাদনে ব্যাকটেরিয়া ব্যবহৃত হয়। ব্যাকটেরিয়ার অর্থনৈতিক গুরুত্ব সম্বন্ধে পরবর্তী অধ্যায়ে বিস্তারিত আলোচনা করা হইয়াছে।\n\n৬.১ ঐতিহাসিক পটভূমিকা (Historical background)\nLeeuwenhoek নামক একজন ওলন্দাজ ১৬৭৬ খৃষ্টাব্দে উহার আবিষ্কারক অণুবীক্ষণযন্ত্রের সাহায্যে প্রথম ব্যাকটেরিয়া অবলোকন করেন। তিনি প্রথম ব্যাকটেরিয়া আবিষ্কার করিলেও উহাদের সম্বন্ধে কোন গুরুত্বপূর্ণ মতবাদ দেন নাই। আগে অনেকেই বলিত ব্যাকটেরিয়া স্বতঃস্ফূর্তভাবে আত্মপ্রকাশ করে। ফরাসি বৈজ্ঞানিক Pasteur, উহা সমর্থন করেন নাই এবং ১৮৬০ খৃষ্টাব্দে প্রমাণ করেন যে, ব্যাকটেরিয়া আপন আপনি হয় না, ব্যাকটেরিয়া হইতে ব্যাকটেরিয়ার সৃষ্টি। পাস্তুরই অজীবজনি (Abiogenesis) মতবাদকে নস্যাৎ করিয়া জীবজনি (Biogenesis) মতবাদ প্রতিষ্ঠা করেন। এইজন্য পাস্তুরকে জীবাণুবিজ্ঞানের (Bacteriology) জনক বলা হয়। পরে, ১৮৮০ খৃষ্টাব্দে Henle নামে একজন জার্মান বিজ্ঞানী জীবাণুজনিত রোগ অনুসন্ধান সম্পর্কে কতগুলি নীতি ঘোষণা করেন। তারপর, তাহার ছাত্র এবং পাস্তুরের নীতিগুলিকে পরিবর্ধন করেন। কোখের নাম অনুসারে এই নীতিগুলিকে বলা হয় কোখ-এর জীবাণু (Koch's postulates)।\n\nনীতিসমূহ চারটি\n\n১। কোনো রোগের জন্য কোনো পরজীবীকে দায়ী মনে করা হইলে উহা ঐ রোগের সঙ্গে সর্বদা সংশ্লিষ্ট থাকিবে।\n২। পরজীবীকে রোগাক্রান্ত কলা হইতে পৃথক করিয়া স্বতন্ত্রভাবে কৃত্রিম আবাদ-মাধ্যমে চাষ করা যাইবে।\n৩। কৃত্রিম আবাদ-মাধ্যম হইতে পরজীবী লইয়া সুস্থ উদ্ভিদে বা প্রাণীদেহে প্রবিষ্ট করা হইলে রোগের বৈশিষ্ট্যপূর্ণ লক্ষণসমূহ প্রকাশ পাইবে।\n৪। রোগগ্রস্ত কলা থেকে পরজীবীকে পুনরায় পৃথক করিয়া আবাদ-মাধ্যমে চাষ করা যাইবে এবং এই পরজীবীর সঙ্গে যে পরজীবীটিকে গাছে বা প্রাণীদেহে প্রবিষ্ট করাইয়া রোগ সৃষ্টি করা হইয়াছিল উহার কোনো পার্থক্য থাকিবে না।\n\nব্যাকটেরিয়া দ্বারা গাছে রোগ হইতে পারে এই সত্যটি প্রথম প্রমাণ করেন যুক্তরাষ্ট্রের ইলিনয়েস বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ের অধ্যাপক T. J. Burrill (১৮৭৮)। পরবর্তীকালে, Walker (১৮৮০), E.F. Smith (১৮৯৫) এবং আরও অনেক গবেষণার মাধ্যমে উদ্ভিদের সঙ্গে ব্যাকটেরিয়ার সম্পর্ক আরও সুদৃঢ় করেন। তাহাদের সকলের উদ্যোগে ব্যাকটেরিয়া আজ বিজ্ঞানের একটি স্বাধীন শাখা হিসাবে পরিচিতি। বিজ্ঞানের যে শাখায় ব্যাকটেরিয়া লইয়া আলোচনা করা হয় উহাকে বলা হয় ব্যাকটেরিওলজি (Bacteriology)।\n\n৬.২ সাধারণ বৈশিষ্ট্য (General characteristics)\n\nব্যাকটেরিয়ার প্রধান বৈশিষ্ট্যগুলি হইলঃ\n১। এককোষী উদ্ভিদ কিন্তু অধিকাংশ ক্ষেত্রে সজুকল্পা অনুপস্থিত।\n২। নিউক্লিয়াস সরল এবং সাইটোপ্লাজমের প্রত্যন্ত অংশে থাকে।\n৩। অণুজীব যা দ্বি-ভাজন পদ্ধতিতে প্রজনন হয়।\n৪। দেহে এক বা একাধিক ফ্লাজেলা (flagella) থাকিতে পারে।\n৫। পরজীবী অথবা মৃতজীবী, বায়ুজীবী অথবা অবায়ুজীবী (anaerobic)।\n৬। কতিপয় প্রজাতি স্পোর উৎপন্ন করে।\n\n৬.২.১ অঙ্গ সংস্থান (Morphology)\nব্যাকটেরিয়ার আয়তন, আকৃতি, কোষবিন্যাস, কোষের গঠন ও সংস্থান উহার অঙ্গ সংস্থানের বিষয়বস্তু।"} {"page_id":"page-0037","text":"২৪\nশস্যের রোগ\n\nইহাদের সম্পর্কে পৃথক পৃথকভাবে নিচে বর্ণনা করা হইল।\n\n১। গঠন (Structure) : ব্যাকটেরিয়ার প্রত্যেকটি কোষই এক একটি গাছের শাখা। কোষের বিভিন্ন অংশের গঠন সম্পর্কে নিচে বর্ণনা করা হইল, (চিত্র-১)\n\n১। কোষপ্রাচীর (Cell wall) : ব্যাকটেরিয়ার কোষপ্রাচীর বেশ শক্ত ও মেমব্রেন জাতীয়। ইহার দুইটি স্তর আছে—প্লাস্টিক স্তর (বাহিরের স্তর এবং অপেক্ষাকৃত নরম) এবং কটিন স্তর (প্লাস্টিক স্তরের নিচে থাকে এবং অপেক্ষাকৃত শক্ত)। কোনো কোনো ব্যাকটেরিয়ায় দুইটি স্তর ও একটি কটিন স্তর থাকে। ইহাতে অতি মাত্রায় মিউকোপেপটাইড থাকে। ইহার বাহিরের স্তরটি সাধারণত লাইপোপ্রোটিন, মিউকোপলিস্যাকারাইড এবং লাইপোপলিস্যাকারাইড দ্বারা গঠিত।\n\n২। স্লাইম স্তর (Slime layer) : কোষপ্রাচীরের বাহিরের যে পিচ্ছিল বা জেলিজাতীয় স্তর আছে উহাকে বলা হয় স্লাইম স্তর। এই স্তরটি গ্যালাকটান, লেভুলান অথবা ডেক্সট্রিনজাতীয় পলিস্যাকারাইড গাম দ্বারা গঠিত। কোনো কোনো ব্যাকটেরিয়ায় এই স্তরটি বেশ মোটা হয় এবং উহাকে বলা হয় ক্যাপসুল। ক্যাপসুল শর্করা পদার্থ দ্বারা তৈরী একটি আঁটাল আবরণ এবং স্লাইম স্তর থেকে উৎপন্ন হয়। সাধারণত প্রতিকূল অবস্থায় যখন ব্যাকটেরিয়া বংশবৃদ্ধি করিতে পারে না, তখন এই ক্যাপসুলের উৎপত্তি হয়। প্রাণীর রোগ উৎপাদক ব্যাকটেরিয়া অধিকাংশ ক্ষেত্রে ক্যাপসুল উৎপন্ন করে।\n\n৩। প্লাজমামেমব্রেন (Plasmamembrane) : কোষপ্রাচীরের ঠিক নিচে সাইটোপ্লাজমকে পরিবেষ্টিত করিয়া পর্দার প্লাজমামেমব্রেন অবস্থিত। ইহার তিনটি স্তর—মাঝখানে লিপিড স্তর এবং উহার উভয় পার্শ্বে প্রোটিনের স্তর। প্লাজমামেমব্রেনকে সহজে রঞ্জিত করা যায়। এই মেমব্রেন জীবকোষের বহিরাগমন-প্রবেশে সহায়ী কিন্তু ইহার ভৌত গঠন ও ধর্ম অর্ধভেদ্য (semi-permeable) পর্দার মতো। অর্থাৎ এই পর্দার মধ্য দিয়া যে কোনো পদার্থ ইচ্ছামত প্রবেশ করিতে বা বাহিরে আসিতে পারে না।\n\n৪। সাইটোপ্লাজম ও উহার অন্তর্ভুক্ত অংশসমূহ (Cytoplasm and its inclusions) : সাইটোপ্লাজম পানিতে বিভিন্ন পদার্থের দ্রবণ দ্বারা উৎপন্ন এক প্রকার কোলয়েড (colloid)। ইহাতে শতকরা ৭৫-৮৫ ভাগ পানি থাকে। ইহার মধ্যে রাইবোসোম নামক গোল দানাদারীয় পদার্থ খুব ঘন করিয়া ছড়ানো থাকে। ইহা প্রোটিন সংশ্লেষণে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ ভূমিকা পালন করে। ইহার মধ্যে আরও থাকে—লিপিড দানা, ভলিউটিন দানা, মেটাক্রোমাটিনের দানা, রাইবোনিউক্লিক এসিড, এনজাইম, বিভিন্ন প্রকারের পিগমেন্টস (pigments), মেসোসোম, র‍্যাফিডোসোম, গহ্বর (vacuole) ইত্যাদি।\n\nব্যাকটেরিয়ার সুসংগঠিত কোনো নিউক্লিয়াস নাই। ইহাতে ক্রোমাটিন জালিকা আছে। এই জালিকা নিউক্লিয়াসের কাজ করে। ব্যাকটেরিয়ার নিউক্লিয়াস মেমব্রেন দ্বারা আবৃত নয়। ফলে, নিউক্লিয়াসের বস্তু সাইটোপ্লাজমে মিশিয়া থাকে। নিউক্লিয়াসের গ্যালাকটান, লেভুলান অথবা ডেক্সট্রিনজাতীয় পলিস্যাকারাইড গাম দ্বারা গঠিত। কোনো কোনো ব্যাকটেরিয়ায় এই স্তরটি বেশ মোটা হয় এবং উহাকে বলা হয় ক্যাপসুল। ক্যাপসুল শর্করা পদার্থ দ্বারা তৈরী একটি আঁটাল আবরণ এবং স্লাইম স্তর থেকে উৎপন্ন হয়। সাধারণত প্রতিকূল অবস্থায় যখন ব্যাকটেরিয়া বংশবৃদ্ধি করিতে পারে না, তখন এই ক্যাপসুলের উৎপত্তি হয়। প্রাণীর রোগ উৎপাদক ব্যাকটেরিয়া অধিকাংশ ক্ষেত্রে ক্যাপসুল উৎপন্ন করে।\n\n৩। প্লাজমামেমব্রেন (Plasmamembrane) : কোষপ্রাচীরের ঠিক নিচে সাইটোপ্লাজমকে পরিবেষ্টিত করিয়া পর্দার প্লাজমামেমব্রেন অবস্থিত। ইহার তিনটি স্তর—মাঝখানে লিপিড স্তর এবং উহার উভয় পার্শ্বে প্রোটিনের স্তর। প্লাজমামেমব্রেনকে সহজে রঞ্জিত করা যায়। এই মেমব্রেন জীবকোষের বহিরাগমন-প্রবেশে সহায়ী কিন্তু ইহার ভৌত গঠন ও ধর্ম অর্ধভেদ্য (semi-permeable) পর্দার মতো। অর্থাৎ এই পর্দার মধ্য দিয়া যে কোনো পদার্থ ইচ্ছামত প্রবেশ করিতে বা বাহিরে আসিতে পারে না।\n\n৪। সাইটোপ্লাজম ও উহার অন্তর্ভুক্ত অংশসমূহ (Cytoplasm and its inclusions) : সাইটোপ্লাজম পানিতে বিভিন্ন পদার্থের দ্রবণ দ্বারা উৎপন্ন এক প্রকার কোলয়েড (colloid)। ইহাতে শতকরা ৭৫-৮৫ ভাগ পানি থাকে। ইহার মধ্যে রাইবোসোম নামক গোল দানাদারীয় পদার্থ খুব ঘন করিয়া ছড়ানো থাকে। ইহা প্রোটিন সংশ্লেষণে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ ভূমিকা পালন করে। ইহার মধ্যে আরও থাকে—লিপিড দানা, ভলিউটিন দানা, মেটাক্রোমাটিনের দানা, রাইবোনিউক্লিক এসিড, এনজাইম, বিভিন্ন প্রকারের পিগমেন্টস (pigments), মেসোসোম, র‍্যাফিডোসোম, গহ্বর (vacuole) ইত্যাদি।\n\nব্যাকটেরিয়ার সুসংগঠিত কোনো নিউক্লিয়াস নাই। ইহাতে ক্রোমাটিন জালিকা আছে। এই জালিকা নিউক্লিয়াসের কাজ করে। ব্যাকটেরিয়ার নিউক্লিয়াস মেমব্রেন দ্বারা আবৃত নয়। ফলে, নিউক্লিয়াসের বস্তু সাইটোপ্লাজমে মিশিয়া থাকে। নিউক্লিয়াসের ক্রোমাটিনগুলি ডি এন এ (DNA) দ্বারা গঠিত এবং একটি ছোট তন্তুর আকারে গাঁথা থাকে। এই মালাটি আংটির মতো গোলাকার এবং ইহাকেই ব্যাকটেরিয়ার ক্রোমোসোম বলিয়া"}
   ]
   with open(LABELS_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
       for item in labels_data:
           f.write(json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Metric Computation Functions (F1, CER, WER)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def normalize_text(text: str) -> str:
   """Normalize unicode and collapse redundant whitespace."""
   text = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text)
   return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()

def levenshtein_distance(seq1: list | str, seq2: list | str) -> int:
   """Standard dynamic programming Levenshtein distance."""
   size_x = len(seq1) + 1
   size_y = len(seq2) + 1
   matrix = np.zeros((size_x, size_y), dtype=int)
   for x in range(size_x):
       matrix[x, 0] = x
   for y in range(size_y):
       matrix[0, y] = y

   for x in range(1, size_x):
       for y in range(1, size_y):
           if seq1[x - 1] == seq2[y - 1]:
               matrix[x, y] = matrix[x - 1, y - 1]
           else:
               matrix[x, y] = min(
                   matrix[x - 1, y] + 1,      # Deletion
                   matrix[x, y - 1] + 1,      # Insertion
                   matrix[x - 1, y - 1] + 1   # Substitution
               )
   return int(matrix[size_x - 1, size_y - 1])

def character_metrics(predicted: str, reference: str) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
   """Calculate character-level precision, recall, and F1 based on multiset overlap."""
   pred_clean = normalize_text(predicted)
   ref_clean = normalize_text(reference)
   pred_counts = Counter(pred_clean)
   ref_counts = Counter(ref_clean)
   
   overlap = sum((pred_counts & ref_counts).values())
   total_pred = max(sum(pred_counts.values()), 1)
   total_ref = max(sum(ref_counts.values()), 1)
   
   precision = overlap / total_pred
   recall = overlap / total_ref
   f1 = (2 * precision * recall) / max(precision + recall, 1e-12)
   return precision, recall, f1

def compute_cer(predicted: str, reference: str) -> float:
   """Compute Character Error Rate (CER)."""
   p = normalize_text(predicted)
   r = normalize_text(reference)
   if not r:
       return 0.0 if not p else 1.0
   return levenshtein_distance(p, r) / len(r)

def compute_wer(predicted: str, reference: str) -> float:
   """Compute Word Error Rate (WER)."""
   p_words = normalize_text(predicted).split()
   r_words = normalize_text(reference).split()
   if not r_words:
       return 0.0 if not p_words else 1.0
   return levenshtein_distance(p_words, r_words) / len(r_words)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Held-Out OCR Evaluation
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
LABELS = [json.loads(line) for line in LABELS_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line.strip()]

OCR_SAMPLE_SIZE = min(3, len(LABELS))
ocr_rows = []

start_ocr_time = time.time()
for label in LABELS[:OCR_SAMPLE_SIZE]:
   page_id = label["page_id"]
   image_path = ROOT / "grading_kit" / "heldout_pages" / f"{page_id}.jpg"
   if not image_path.is_file():
       image_path = ROOT / "grading_kit" / "heldout_pages" / f"{page_id}.png"
       
   page = Page(id=page_id, doc_id="krishipath-heldout", image_path=str(image_path))
   page_cfg = dict(CFG)
   
   # If the held-out image doesn't exist on disk yet, skip gracefully
   if not Path(page.image_path).is_file():
       print(f"Skipping {page_id}: Image not found at {page.image_path}")
       continue
       
   regions = layout.detect([page], page_cfg)
   prediction = "\n".join(chunk.text for chunk in ocr.transcribe(regions, page_cfg))
   
   prec, rec, f1 = character_metrics(prediction, label["text"])
   cer = compute_cer(prediction, label["text"])
   wer = compute_wer(prediction, label["text"])
   
   ocr_rows.append({
       "page_id": page_id,
       "regions": len(regions),
       "precision": prec,
       "recall": rec,
       "f1": f1,
       "cer": cer,
       "wer": wer,
   })

total_ocr_latency = time.time() - start_ocr_time
if ocr_rows:
   mean_prec = float(np.mean([r["precision"] for r in ocr_rows]))
   mean_rec = float(np.mean([r["recall"] for r in ocr_rows]))
   mean_f1 = float(np.mean([r["f1"] for r in ocr_rows]))
   mean_cer = float(np.mean([r["cer"] for r in ocr_rows]))
   mean_wer = float(np.mean([r["wer"] for r in ocr_rows]))

   display(Markdown(f"""
## Held-out OCR Quality & Error Metrics
- **Evaluated Samples:** `{len(ocr_rows)}` pages (Total latency: `{total_ocr_latency:.2f}s`)
- **Mean Character Precision:** `{mean_prec:.4f}`
- **Mean Character Recall:** `{mean_rec:.4f}`
- **Mean Character F1:** **`{mean_f1:.4f}`**
- **Mean CER (Character Error Rate):** `{mean_cer:.4f}` ({mean_cer * 100:.2f}%)
- **Mean WER (Word Error Rate):** `{mean_wer:.4f}` ({mean_wer * 100:.2f}%)
   """))

   print(f"{'Page ID':<12} | {'Regions':<8} | {'Precision':<10} | {'Recall':<10} | {'Char F1':<10} | {'CER':<10} | {'WER':<10}")
   print("-" * 80)
   for row in ocr_rows:
       print(f"{row['page_id']:<12} | {row['regions']:<8} | {row['precision']:<10.4f} | {row['recall']:<10.4f} | {row['f1']:<10.4f} | {row['cer']:<10.4f} | {row['wer']:<10.4f}")
else:
   print("Warning: No OCR evaluation performed. Ensure heldout_pages/ directory contains the images.")

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Knowledge Base Inspection & Index Statistics
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
index_dir = ROOT / CFG.get("index", {}).get("path", "data/index")
if not (index_dir / "index.faiss").is_file():
   print("Index missing on disk; building knowledge base...")
   pipeline.build_knowledge_base(CFG)

faiss_index, indexed_chunks = store.load(CFG)
metadata = json.loads((index_dir / "metadata.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
indexed_pages = {page_id for chunk in indexed_chunks for page_id in chunk.page_ids}
word_counts = [len(chunk.text.split()) for chunk in indexed_chunks]

display(Markdown(f"""
## Knowledge Base & Vector Index Statistics
- **Index Type:** `{metadata.get('index_type', 'faiss:hnsw')}` (`{metadata.get('metric', 'inner_product')}`)
- **Embedding Model:** `{CFG.get('embed', {}).get('model', 'BAAI/bge-m3')}` (Dimension: `{metadata.get('dimension', faiss_index.d)}`)
- **Total Chunks Indexed:** **`{len(indexed_chunks):,}`**
- **Source Pages Represented:** **`{len(indexed_pages):,} / 378`**
- **HNSW Parameters:** `M={metadata.get('hnsw_m', 32)}`, `efConstruction={metadata.get('ef_construction', 40)}`, `efSearch={metadata.get('ef_search', 64)}`
- **Mean Chunk Length:** `{np.mean(word_counts):.1f} words` (Min: `{np.min(word_counts)}`, Max: `{np.max(word_counts)}`)
"""))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Semantic Retrieval Demonstration
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

QUERY = "কোখ-এর জীবাণু নীতিসমূহ কতটি?"
EXPECTED_PAGE = "page-0036"
TOP_K = 3

embed_cfg = CFG.get("embed", {})
device = "cuda" if str(embed_cfg.get("device", "cpu")).startswith("cuda") else "cpu"
embedder = SentenceTransformer(embed_cfg.get("model", "BAAI/bge-m3"), device=device)

start_search = time.time()
query_vector = embedder.encode([QUERY], normalize_embeddings=True, convert_to_numpy=True).astype("float32")
scores, positions = faiss_index.search(query_vector, k=TOP_K)
search_latency_ms = (time.time() - start_search) * 1000

results = [
   (indexed_chunks[int(position)], float(score))
   for score, position in zip(scores[0], positions[0])
   if position >= 0
]

top_1_pages = set(results[0][0].page_ids) if results else set()
hit_at_1 = EXPECTED_PAGE in top_1_pages

reciprocal_rank = 0.0
for rank, (result, _) in enumerate(results, start=1):
   if EXPECTED_PAGE in result.page_ids:
       reciprocal_rank = 1.0 / rank
       break

display(Markdown(f"""
## Retrieval Demonstration & Ground-Truth Verification
- **Query:** `{QUERY}`
- **Target Ground Truth Page:** `{EXPECTED_PAGE}`
- **Search Latency:** `{search_latency_ms:.2f} ms`
- **Hit@1:** **`{'PASS (True)' if hit_at_1 else 'FAIL (False)'}`**
- **Reciprocal Rank (RR):** `{reciprocal_rank:.4f}`
"""))

for rank, (result, score) in enumerate(results, start=1):
   excerpt = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", result.text).strip()
   preview = excerpt[:250] + ("..." if len(excerpt) > 250 else "")
   print(f"#{rank:<2} | Score={score:.4f} | Pages={result.page_ids} | Chunk={result.id}")
   print(f"    Excerpt: {preview}")
   print("-" * 88)

print(f"Retrieval success at rank 1: {hit_at_1}")

Yes. The error is very specific: your RTX 5060 Ti is Blackwell (sm_120), but the PyTorch binary actually installed in your venv does not contain sm_120 kernels.

Your own warning confirms it:

RTX 5060 Ti with CUDA capability sm_120 is not compatible

current PyTorch install supports ... sm_90

So this is not primarily a problem with TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST, and you don't need to rebuild your project. Your installed PyTorch wheel is the problem.

PyTorch's CUDA 12.8 builds support Blackwell/sm_120; this has been supported in stable PyTorch since the 2.7-era CUDA 12.8 builds. (PyTorch Forums)

1. First, check exactly what you installed

Inside your .venv, run:

python -c "import torch; print('Torch:', torch.__version__); print('CUDA:', torch.version.cuda); print('Arch:', torch.cuda.get_arch_list()); print('GPU:', torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)); print('Capability:', torch.cuda.get_device_capability(0))"

I expect your output will show something like:

Torch: ...
CUDA: 12.8
Arch: ['sm_50', ..., 'sm_90']
GPU: NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti
Capability: (12, 0)

The important part is that sm_120 will be missing.

2. I recommend using stable PyTorch CUDA 12.8

You don't actually need nightly anymore.

Your RTX 5060 Ti is supported by stable PyTorch CUDA 12.8 builds. (PyTorch Forums)

Since you're using uv, do a clean uninstall:

uv pip uninstall torch torchvision torchaudio

Then install the CUDA 12.8 versions:

uv pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu128

Do not use --prerelease allow here.

3. Verify BEFORE running your project

This is important. Don't immediately run build_index.sh.

Run:

python -c "import torch; print(torch.__version__); print(torch.version.cuda); print(torch.cuda.get_arch_list()); print(torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)); print(torch.cuda.get_device_capability(0)); print(torch.randn(10,10,device='cuda').sum())"

You want to see something along these lines:

2.x.x+cu128
12.8
['sm_50', ..., 'sm_90', 'sm_100', 'sm_120', ...]
NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti
(12, 0)
tensor(...)

The critical test is:

sm_120

being present in torch.cuda.get_arch_list().

And this:

torch.randn(10,10,device='cuda')

must succeed.

PyTorch specifically recommends this kind of standalone smoke test when diagnosing Blackwell compatibility. (PyTorch Forums)

4. If sm_120 is STILL missing

Then uv may be resolving an unexpected/cached wheel.

Run:

uv pip show torch

and:

python -c "import torch; print(torch.__file__); print(torch.__version__); print(torch.version.cuda); print(torch.cuda.get_arch_list())"

Also:

uv pip list | grep -E 'torch|vision|audio'

You should have all three packages coming from the same CUDA 12.8 generation.

If necessary, force a fresh installation:

uv pip uninstall torch torchvision torchaudio
uv cache clean
uv pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu128

Then repeat the smoke test.

5. Your TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST is not fixing this

You currently have:

export TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST="12.0+PTX"

That variable is mainly relevant when compiling CUDA extensions/source code.

It does not magically add sm_120 kernels to an already-built PyTorch wheel.

So this:

export TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST="12.0+PTX"

cannot repair a PyTorch binary that was compiled without the necessary architecture.

You can leave it unset for now:

unset TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST

Keep:

export CUDA_MODULE_LOADING=LAZY

if you want, although it isn't the solution to this particular error.

6. Then run your ingestion again

Once this succeeds:

python -c "import torch; print(torch.cuda.get_arch_list()); print(torch.randn(10,10,device='cuda').sum())"

run:

PYTHONPATH="$PWD/src" bash scripts/build_index.sh
python scripts/run_ingest.py

def build_knowledge_base(cfg: dict) -> None:
    wiring.register_all(cfg)                        # wire cross-cutting features
    pages = loader.load_pages(cfg)
    pages = preprocess.run(pages, cfg)
    pages = enhance.run(pages, cfg)                 # Stage 1 - enhancement (VAE/diffusion)
    hooks.run(hooks.AFTER_INGEST, {"pages": pages})
    regions = layout.detect(pages, cfg)             # Stage 2
    text = ocr.transcribe(regions, cfg)             # Stage 3
    hooks.run(hooks.AFTER_OCR, {"chunks": text})    # e.g. PII redaction on extracted text
    chunks = chunk.split(text, cfg)                 # Stage 4
    hooks.run(hooks.BEFORE_INDEX, {"chunks": chunks})
    vectors = embed.encode(chunks, cfg)
    store.build(chunks, vectors, cfg)

"""Stage 1 -- page preprocessing before layout detection and OCR."""

from future import annotations

import logging
import math
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageOps

from ..contracts import Page

LOGGER = logging.getLogger(name)

def _estimate_text_line_skew(image: np.ndarray) -> float | None:
    """Estimate skew from long, near-horizontal text lines."""
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY)
    edges = cv2.Canny(cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (3, 3), 0), 50, 150)
    _, width = gray.shape
    lines = cv2.HoughLinesP(
        edges,
        rho=1,
        theta=np.pi / 360,
        threshold=max(35, width // 18),
        minLineLength=max(80, width // 7),
        maxLineGap=max(8, width // 120),
    )
    if lines is None:
        return None

angles: list[float] = []
for x1, y1, x2, y2 in lines.reshape(-1, 4):
    if math.hypot(float(x2 - x1), float(y2 - y1)) < width * 0.12:
        continue
    angle = math.degrees(math.atan2(float(y2 - y1), float(x2 - x1)))
    if abs(angle) <= 2.0:
        angles.append(angle)
if len(angles) < 6:
    return None

median = float(np.median(angles))
inliers = [angle for angle in angles if abs(angle - median) <= 0.25]
return float(np.median(inliers)) if len(inliers) >= 6 else None

def _trim(image: np.ndarray, trim_left_px: int, trim_border_px: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Apply configured edge trims."""
    height, width = image.shape[:2]
    left = trim_left_px + trim_border_px
    top = trim_border_px
    right = width - trim_border_px
    bottom = height - trim_border_px
    if left >= right - 16 or top >= bottom - 16:
        raise ValueError("Configured preprocessing trim leaves an invalid image.")
    return image[top:bottom, left:right]

def _preprocess_image(image: np.ndarray, settings: dict[str, float | int | bool]) -> np.ndarray:
    """Apply configured edge trimming and deskewing."""
    image = _trim(image, int(settings["trim_left_px"]), int(settings["trim_border_px"]))
    if not bool(settings["deskew"]):
        return image

skew = _estimate_text_line_skew(image)
if skew is None:
    return image
if not float(settings["min_skew_degrees"]) <= abs(skew) <= float(settings["max_skew_degrees"]):
    return image
height, width = image.shape[:2]
matrix = cv2.getRotationMatrix2D((width / 2, height / 2), -skew, 1.0)
return cv2.warpAffine(
    image,
    matrix,
    (width, height),
    flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC,
    borderMode=cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT,
    borderValue=(255, 255, 255),
)

def _settings(cfg: dict) -> dict[str, float | int | bool | Path]:
    configured = cfg.get("preprocess", {})
    return {
        "enabled": bool(configured.get("enabled", True)),
        "output_dir": Path(configured.get("output_dir", "data/interim/preprocessed")),
        "trim_left_px": max(0, int(configured.get("trim_left_px", 80))),
        "trim_border_px": max(0, int(configured.get("trim_border_px", 0))),
        "deskew": bool(configured.get("deskew", True)),
        "min_skew_degrees": max(0.0, float(configured.get("min_skew_degrees", 0.15))),
        "max_skew_degrees": max(0.0, float(configured.get("max_skew_degrees", 2.0))),
    }

def run(pages: list[Page], cfg: dict) -> list[Page]:
    """Write preprocessed images without modifying the raw corpus."""
    settings = _settings(cfg)
    if not bool(settings["enabled"]):
        return pages

output_dir = Path(settings["output_dir"])
processed_pages: list[Page] = []
for page in pages:
    source_path = Path(page.image_path)
    if not source_path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Page image does not exist: {source_path}")
    with Image.open(source_path) as source:
        image = np.asarray(ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert("RGB"))
    output_path = output_dir / Path(page.id).with_suffix(".png")
    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    Image.fromarray(_preprocess_image(image, settings)).save(
        output_path, format="PNG", optimize=True
    )
    processed_pages.append(page.model_copy(update={"image_path": str(output_path)}))

LOGGER.info("Preprocessed %d page(s) into %s", len(processed_pages), output_dir)
return processed_pages

AI: 
15/08/2026, 22:37:26

Yes. Now we can fix it precisely.

Your pii.py is a starter stub, and your current A2 run is failing because register() is wiring the PII hook, but _scrub() is still deliberately unimplemented.

The important part is that the hook receives different kinds of contexts:

AFTER_OCR   -> {"chunks": text}
BEFORE_ANSWER -> likely {"answer": ...}
ON_LOG      -> log-related context

So we should not simply do ctx["chunks"] = redact(ctx["chunks"]), because chunks is almost certainly a list/object structure rather than a plain string.

Since this is your course's regulated codebase, we should preserve the existing function signatures and hook locations. The codebase guide specifically says PII is wired through governance/pii.py, while the pipeline's OCR output then goes through the AFTER_OCR hook before chunking/indexing. 05-Codebase-Guide.md

Replace pii.py with this

"""Governance - PII detection + redaction (mandatory)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from ..contracts import *  # noqa

# Common PII patterns.
_PATTERNS: list[tuple[str, re.Pattern[str]]] = [
    (
        "email",
        re.compile(
            r"\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "phone",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)(?:\+?880[-.\s]?)?"
            r"(?:01[3-9][-\s]?\d{3}[-\s]?\d{4}|"
            r"\d{3}[-.\s]\d{3}[-.\s]\d{4})"
            r"(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "nid",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)\d{10,17}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "credit_card",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
]

def detect(text: str) -> list[tuple[int, int, str]]:
    """Return (start, end, type) PII spans."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return []

    spans: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []

    for pii_type, pattern in _PATTERNS:
        for match in pattern.finditer(text):
            spans.append((match.start(), match.end(), pii_type))

    # Remove overlapping duplicates while preserving source order.
    spans.sort(key=lambda span: (span[0], span[1]))

    result: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []
    last_end = -1

    for start, end, pii_type in spans:
        if start >= last_end:
            result.append((start, end, pii_type))
            last_end = end

    return result

def redact(text: str) -> str:
    """Replace detected PII spans with typed redaction markers."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return text

    spans = detect(text)

    if not spans:
        return text

    parts: list[str] = []
    cursor = 0

    for start, end, pii_type in spans:
        parts.append(text[cursor:start])
        parts.append(f"[REDACTED_{pii_type.upper()}]")
        cursor = end

    parts.append(text[cursor:])

    return "".join(parts)

def _redact_value(value):
    """Recursively redact strings inside common pipeline structures."""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return redact(value)

    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_redact_value(item) for item in value]

    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return tuple(_redact_value(item) for item in value)

    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {
            key: _redact_value(item)
            for key, item in value.items()
        }

    # Handle Pydantic-style objects without changing their type.
    if hasattr(value, "model_copy"):
        updates = {}

        for field_name in getattr(value, "model_fields", {}):
            try:
                field_value = getattr(value, field_name)
            except Exception:
                continue

            redacted_value = _redact_value(field_value)

            if redacted_value != field_value:
                updates[field_name] = redacted_value

        if updates:
            return value.model_copy(update=updates)

    return value

def register(hooks) -> None:
    """Wire PII redaction into the pipeline."""

    def _scrub(ctx: dict) -> dict:
        if not isinstance(ctx, dict):
            return ctx

        return {
            key: _redact_value(value)
            for key, value in ctx.items()
        }

    hooks.register(hooks.AFTER_OCR, _scrub)
    hooks.register(hooks.BEFORE_ANSWER, _scrub)
    hooks.register(hooks.ON_LOG, _scrub)

Why this version fits your current failure

Your pipeline does:

text = ocr.transcribe(regions, cfg)

hooks.run(
    hooks.AFTER_OCR,
    {"chunks": text}
)

chunks = chunk.split(text, cfg)

Therefore, the PII hook needs to transform the OCR result before it reaches chunking/indexing. That's exactly where your exception occurred.

The course guide explicitly describes the OCR stage as producing text chunks and the index stage as consuming/chunking/embedding them. 05-Codebase-Guide.md

print("Detected:")
print(detect(text))

print("\nRedacted:")

redact(text: str) -> str

and doesn't tell us the exact PII categories/recall requirements. The project specification says PII is an NFR-gated governance capability, with a concrete target such as PII recall ≥ 0.98 when that NFR is selected. 04-Project-Walkthrough(1).pdf

So the implementation above is enough to remove the current NotImplementedError and give you a functional baseline, but before submission we should check your team's selected NFR and the PII tests/contract and make the detector match those requirements rather than guessing what the grader expects.

import re
from ..contracts import *  # noqa

_PATTERNS = [
    (
        "email",
        re.compile(
            r"\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "phone",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)(?:\+?880[-.\s]?)?"
            r"(?:01[3-9][-\s]?\d{3}[-\s]?\d{4})"
            r"(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "credit_card",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "nid",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)\d{10,17}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
]

def detect(text: str) -> list[tuple[int, int, str]]:
    """Return (start, end, type) PII spans."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return []

    spans = []

    for pii_type, pattern in _PATTERNS:
        for match in pattern.finditer(text):
            spans.append((match.start(), match.end(), pii_type))

    spans.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))

    result = []
    last_end = -1

    for start, end, pii_type in spans:
        if start >= last_end:
            result.append((start, end, pii_type))
            last_end = end

    return result

def redact(text: str) -> str:
    """Replace detected PII spans with redaction markers."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return text

    spans = detect(text)

    if not spans:
        return text

    result = []
    cursor = 0

    for start, end, pii_type in spans:
        result.append(text[cursor:start])
        result.append(f"[REDACTED_{pii_type.upper()}]")
        cursor = end

    result.append(text[cursor:])

    return "".join(result)

def _redact_value(value):
    """Recursively redact strings contained in pipeline data."""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return redact(value)

    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_redact_value(item) for item in value]

    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return tuple(_redact_value(item) for item in value)

    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {
            key: _redact_value(item)
            for key, item in value.items()

    if hasattr(value, "model_copy"):
        updates = {}

            try:

            except Exception:

            if redacted_value != field_value:
                updates[field_name] = redacted_value

        if updates:
            return value.model_copy(update=updates)

    return value

def register(hooks) -> None:
    """Wire PII redaction into the pipeline."""

    def _scrub(ctx: dict) -> dict:
        if not isinstance(ctx, dict):
            return ctx

        return {
            key: _redact_value(value)
            for key, value in ctx.items()

    hooks.register(hooks.AFTER_OCR, _scrub)
    hooks.register(hooks.BEFORE_ANSWER, _scrub)
    hooks.register(hooks.ON_LOG, _scrub)

Good - the PII module is now being loaded correctly, and detect()/redact() are working.

But there is one bug in the implementation we should fix before rerunning the 378-page A2 build:

+880 1712345678

is being detected as:

nid

instead of:

phone

because the generic NID regex:

\d{10,17}

is matching the 1712345678 portion of the phone number.

Fix the pattern ordering/overlap

In pii.py, change your _PATTERNS section so the phone pattern is more specific and the NID pattern doesn't capture digits that are part of a phone number.

Use:

_PATTERNS = [
    (
        "email",
        re.compile(
            r"\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "phone",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)"
            r"(?:\+?880[\s.-]?)?"
            r"01[3-9][\s.-]?\d{3}[\s.-]?\d{4}"
            r"(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "credit_card",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "nid",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<![\d+])\d{10,17}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
]

Your overlap-resolution code already processes spans in order, so the phone match will take precedence over the generic numeric match.

"""Governance — PII detection + redaction (mandatory)."""
from future import annotations

import re
from ..contracts import *  # noqa

Common PII patterns.

PATTERNS: list[tuple[str, re.Pattern[str]]] = [
    (
        "email",
        re.compile(
            r"\b[A-Za-z0-9.%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "phone",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)(?:+?880[-.\s]?)?"
            r"(?:01[3-9][-\s]?\d{3}[-\s]?\d{4}|"
            r"\d{3}[-.\s]\d{3}[-.\s]\d{4})"
            r"(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "nid",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)\d{10,17}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "credit_card",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
]

def detect(text: str) -> list[tuple[int, int, str]]:
    """Return (start, end, type) PII spans."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return []

spans: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []

for pii_type, pattern in _PATTERNS:
    for match in pattern.finditer(text):
        spans.append((match.start(), match.end(), pii_type))

# Remove overlapping duplicates while preserving source order.
spans.sort(key=lambda span: (span[0], span[1]))

result: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []
last_end = -1

for start, end, pii_type in spans:
    if start >= last_end:
        result.append((start, end, pii_type))
        last_end = end

return result

def redact(text: str) -> str:
    """Replace detected PII spans with typed redaction markers."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return text

spans = detect(text)

if not spans:
    return text

parts: list[str] = []
cursor = 0

for start, end, pii_type in spans:
    parts.append(text[cursor:start])
    parts.append(f"[REDACTED_{pii_type.upper()}]")
    cursor = end

parts.append(text[cursor:])

return "".join(parts)

def _redact_value(value):
    """Recursively redact strings inside common pipeline structures."""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return redact(value)

if isinstance(value, list):
    return [_redact_value(item) for item in value]

if isinstance(value, tuple):
    return tuple(_redact_value(item) for item in value)

if isinstance(value, dict):
    return {
        key: _redact_value(item)
        for key, item in value.items()
    }

# Handle Pydantic-style objects without changing their type.
if hasattr(value, "model_copy"):
    updates = {}

    for field_name in getattr(value, "model_fields", {}):
        try:
            field_value = getattr(value, field_name)
        except Exception:
            continue

        redacted_value = _redact_value(field_value)

        if redacted_value != field_value:
            updates[field_name] = redacted_value

    if updates:
        return value.model_copy(update=updates)

return value

def register(hooks) -> None:
    """Wire PII redaction into the pipeline."""

def _scrub(ctx: dict) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(ctx, dict):
        return ctx

    return {
        key: _redact_value(value)
        for key, value in ctx.items()
    }

hooks.register(hooks.AFTER_OCR, _scrub)
hooks.register(hooks.BEFORE_ANSWER, _scrub)
hooks.register(hooks.ON_LOG, _scrub)

Yes. Replace the entire contents of src/doc_agent/governance/pii.py with this:

"""Governance — PII detection + redaction (mandatory)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from ..contracts import *  # noqa

# Common PII patterns.
_PATTERNS: list[tuple[str, re.Pattern[str]]] = [
    (
        "email",
        re.compile(
            r"\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "phone",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)"
            r"(?:\+?880[\s.-]?)?"
            r"01[3-9][\s.-]?\d{3}[\s.-]?\d{4}"
            r"(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "credit_card",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<!\d)"
            r"(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}"
            r"(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
    (
        "nid",
        re.compile(
            r"(?<![\d+])"
            r"\d{10,17}"
            r"(?!\d)"
        ),
    ),
]

def detect(text: str) -> list[tuple[int, int, str]]:
    """Return (start, end, type) PII spans."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return []

    spans: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []

    for pii_type, pattern in _PATTERNS:
        for match in pattern.finditer(text):
            spans.append(
                (match.start(), match.end(), pii_type)
            )

    # Sort by position in the original text.
    spans.sort(key=lambda span: (span[0], span[1]))

    # Remove overlapping matches.
    # Earlier/more-specific matches take precedence.
    result: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []
    last_end = -1

    for start, end, pii_type in spans:
        if start >= last_end:
            result.append((start, end, pii_type))
            last_end = end

    return result

def redact(text: str) -> str:
    """Replace detected PII spans with typed redaction markers."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        return text

    spans = detect(text)

    if not spans:
        return text

    parts: list[str] = []
    cursor = 0

    for start, end, pii_type in spans:
        parts.append(text[cursor:start])
        parts.append(f"[REDACTED_{pii_type.upper()}]")
        cursor = end

    parts.append(text[cursor:])

    return "".join(parts)

def _redact_value(value):
    """Recursively redact strings contained in pipeline data."""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return redact(value)

    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_redact_value(item) for item in value]

    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return tuple(_redact_value(item) for item in value)

    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {
            key: _redact_value(item)
            for key, item in value.items()
        }

    # Handle Pydantic-style objects without changing their type.
    if hasattr(value, "model_copy"):
        updates = {}

        for field_name in getattr(value, "model_fields", {}):
            try:
                field_value = getattr(value, field_name)
            except Exception:
                continue

            redacted_value = _redact_value(field_value)

            if redacted_value != field_value:
                updates[field_name] = redacted_value

        if updates:
            return value.model_copy(update=updates)

    return value

def register(hooks) -> None:
    """Wire PII redaction into the pipeline."""

    def _scrub(ctx: dict) -> dict:
        if not isinstance(ctx, dict):
            return ctx

        return {
            key: _redact_value(value)
            for key, value in ctx.items()
        }

    hooks.register(hooks.AFTER_OCR, _scrub)
    hooks.register(hooks.BEFORE_ANSWER, _scrub)
    hooks.register(hooks.ON_LOG, _scrub)

Then test it before running A2

]

for text in tests:
    print("\nOriginal :", text)
    print("Detected :", detect(text))

Yes — this explains the long wait. The 2.27 GB download is your BGE-M3 embedding model, not the DocLayout/OCR model.

Your config has:

embed:
  model: "BAAI/bge-m3"
  dim: 1024

BAAI/bge-m3 is a large embedding model, and the line:

pytorch_model.bin: 0%| | 0.00/2.27G

means your A2 pipeline has reached the embedding stage and is downloading the model weights.

This is actually good news

Your pipeline has successfully passed the point where it previously crashed:

preprocess
   ↓
layout
   ↓
OCR
   ↓
PII hook       ✅ fixed
   ↓
chunking
   ↓
BGE-M3         ← YOU ARE HERE
   ↓
FAISS index

So your previous PII problem is apparently fixed.

Why is it 2.27 GB?

Because you're using:

embed:
  model: "BAAI/bge-m3"

BGE-M3 is a fairly large multilingual embedding model. Your project needs it to convert the chunks extracted from your agricultural document into vectors before putting them into FAISS.

The model download includes files such as:

tokenizer_config.json
sentencepiece.bpe.model
tokenizer.json
...
pytorch_model.bin       ← 2.27 GB

The 2.27 GB is the model weights.

It's not downloading 2.27 GB for every page. It's a one-time model download.

Why has it taken an hour?

The key line is:

pytorch_model.bin: 0% | 0.00/2.27G

If it has genuinely remained at:

0.00/2.27G

for an hour, that's not normal.

At this point I would not just keep waiting blindly.

Open another WSL terminal and run:

ps aux | grep -E 'python|uv' | grep -v grep

Then:

nvidia-smi

And check your network/download process:

du -sh ~/.cache/huggingface 2>/dev/null

Run that last command twice, perhaps 30 seconds apart.

If the cache size is increasing, the download is progressing even though the progress display isn't updating.

Also check the actual download speed

Run:

ls -lh ~/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--BAAI--bge-m3/*/* 2>/dev/null

or simply:

find ~/.cache/huggingface -type f -printf '%s %p\n' 2>/dev/null | sort -nr | head

If you see a large file growing, leave it running.

One important thing about your setup

You're running the project from:

/mnt/e/BUET L4T1/CSE429/doc-agent-G16

which is a Windows-mounted filesystem inside WSL.

Your Hugging Face cache, however, is probably under:

~/.cache/huggingface

which should normally be on the Linux filesystem.

That's good — the model download itself shouldn't be suffering from /mnt/e filesystem performance unless you've configured the cache there.

How long should 2.27 GB take?

Very roughly:

Download speed

import torch

and:

def _resolve_device(device: str) -> str:
    if device == "auto":
        return "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    return device

device = _resolve_device(device)
_MODEL_CACHE[key] = SentenceTransformer(model_name, device=device)

Me::
15/08/2026, 23:06:24

embed.py:
"""Stage 4 - encode chunks as normalized dense vectors."""
from future import annotations

from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from ..contracts import Chunk

_MODEL_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, str], Any] = {}

def _device(cfg: dict) -> str:
    """Select a usable torch device without requiring CUDA on every machine."""
    requested = str(cfg.get("embed", {}).get("device", cfg.get("device", "cpu")))
    if requested.startswith("cuda"):
        try:
            import torch

if torch.cuda.is_available():
            return requested
    except ImportError:
        pass
    return "cpu"
return requested

def _get_model(cfg: dict) -> Any:
    try:
        from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Embedding requires sentence-transformers. Install the project dependencies."
        ) from exc

embed_cfg = cfg.get("embed", {})
model_name = str(embed_cfg.get("model", "BAAI/bge-m3"))
device = _device(cfg)
key = (model_name, device)
if key not in _MODEL_CACHE:
    _MODEL_CACHE[key] = SentenceTransformer(model_name, device=device)
return _MODEL_CACHE[key]

def encode(chunks: list[Chunk], cfg: dict) -> np.ndarray:
    """Encode chunks with the configured embedding model.

Embeddings are L2-normalized so inner-product FAISS indexes behave as
cosine-similarity indexes. The returned array is always float32 with shape
``(number_of_chunks, configured_dimension)``.
"""
embed_cfg = cfg.get("embed", {})
expected_dim = int(embed_cfg.get("dim", 1024))
if expected_dim <= 0:
    raise ValueError("embed.dim must be positive")

if not chunks:
    return np.empty((0, expected_dim), dtype=np.float32)

model = _get_model(cfg)
actual_dim = model.get_sentence_embedding_dimension()
if actual_dim != expected_dim:
    raise ValueError(
        f"Embedding dimension mismatch: config requests {expected_dim}, "
        f"but the model produces {actual_dim}."
    )

batch_size = int(embed_cfg.get("batch_size", 16))
if batch_size <= 0:
    raise ValueError("embed.batch_size must be positive")

texts = [chunk.text for chunk in chunks]
vectors = model.encode(
    texts,
    batch_size=batch_size,
    show_progress_bar=bool(embed_cfg.get("show_progress", False)),
    convert_to_numpy=True,
    normalize_embeddings=True,
)
vectors = np.asarray(vectors, dtype=np.float32)
if vectors.shape != (len(chunks), expected_dim):
    raise ValueError(
        f"Unexpected embedding shape {vectors.shape}; "
        f"expected {(len(chunks), expected_dim)}."
    )
return vectors

AI: 
15/08/2026, 23:06:24

Yes. Your embed.py shows the exact problem.

The current _device() handles cuda and cpu, but doesn't handle auto. With your config:

device: auto

this line:

requested = str(cfg.get("embed", {}).get("device", cfg.get("device", "cpu")))

produces:

requested = "auto"

and then returns "auto" unchanged. SentenceTransformer passes that to PyTorch, which fails.

Make this exact change

Replace your _device() function:

def _device(cfg: dict) -> str:
    """Select a usable torch device without requiring CUDA on every machine."""
    requested = str(cfg.get("embed", {}).get("device", cfg.get("device", "cpu")))
    if requested.startswith("cuda"):
        try:
            import torch

            if torch.cuda.is_available():
                return requested
        except ImportError:
            pass
        return "cpu"
    return requested

with:

def _device(cfg: dict) -> str:
    """Select a usable torch device without requiring CUDA on every machine."""
    requested = str(
        cfg.get("embed", {}).get(
            "device",
            cfg.get("device", "cpu"),
        )
    ).lower()

    try:
        import torch
    except ImportError:
        if requested in {"auto", "cpu"}:
            return "cpu"
        return "cpu"

    if requested == "auto":
        return "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"

    if requested.startswith("cuda"):
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            return requested
        return "cpu"

    if requested == "cpu":
        return "cpu"

    return requested

Why this is the correct fix

With your current config:

device: auto

it will now do:

auto
 ↓
torch.cuda.is_available()
 ↓
True  → cuda
False → cpu

So you don't need to change your configuration just because you're running on a different machine.

But check your RTX 5060 Ti first

Because earlier your PyTorch installation reported:

RTX 5060 Ti with CUDA capability sm_120 is not compatible

you should verify the new cu128 installation before running the full embedding stage.

Run:

python - <<'PY'
import torch

print("PyTorch:", torch.__version__)
print("CUDA available:", torch.cuda.is_available())
print("CUDA version:", torch.version.cuda)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0))
    print("Capability:", torch.cuda.get_device_capability(0))
PY

If you get:

PyTorch: 2.11.0+cu128
CUDA available: True
CUDA version: 12.8
GPU: NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti
Capability: (12, 0)

then you're good to use auto, and it will resolve to CUDA.

If CUDA is unavailable, it will automatically fall back to CPU.

Your BGE-M3 download is already done

This part:

pytorch_model.bin: 100%|██████████| 2.27G/2.27G

means the model is now cached. After fixing _device(), you should not have to download that 2.27 GB again.

Your pipeline got to:

Chunking                ✅
BGE-M3 download         ✅
BGE-M3 loading          ❌ "auto"

So this is a relatively small fix.

If build_index.sh invokes run_ingest.py, don't separately run python scripts/run_ingest.py afterward.

The only expensive stage will be BGE-M3 embedding, followed by FAISS indexing.

Standalone script

Create:

nano scripts/build_index_from_ocr.py

Put this entire file in it:

"""Build the FAISS index directly from an existing OCR JSONL file."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
import faiss

# ============================================================
# Configuration
# ============================================================

OCR_FILE = Path("data/interim/ocr_pages.jsonl")

INDEX_DIR = Path("data/index")

MODEL_NAME = "BAAI/bge-m3"
EMBED_DIM = 1024

# Same chunking configuration as config.yaml
CHUNK_TOKENS = 256
OVERLAP = 32

BATCH_SIZE = 16

# ============================================================
# Load OCR
# ============================================================

def load_ocr_pages(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    """Load OCR page records from JSONL."""

    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"OCR file not found: {path}"
        )

    pages: list[dict] = []

    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line_number, line in enumerate(f, start=1):
            line = line.strip()

            if not line:
                continue

            try:
                record = json.loads(line)
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Invalid JSON on line {line_number}: {exc}"
                ) from exc

            if not isinstance(record, dict):
                continue

            text = record.get("text", "")

            if not isinstance(text, str):
                continue

            text = text.strip()

            if not text:
                continue

            pages.append(record)

    return pages

# ============================================================
# Chunking
# ============================================================

def chunk_text(
    text: str,
    chunk_tokens: int = CHUNK_TOKENS,
    overlap: int = OVERLAP,
) -> list[str]:
    """
    Create approximately token-sized chunks.

    We use whitespace-separated units here rather than loading
    another tokenizer. BGE-M3 will perform its own tokenization
    during embedding.
    """

    words = text.split()

    if not words:
        return []

    if overlap >= chunk_tokens:
        raise ValueError(
            "overlap must be smaller than chunk_tokens"
        )

    chunks: list[str] = []

    step = chunk_tokens - overlap

    for start in range(0, len(words), step):
        end = min(start + chunk_tokens, len(words))

        chunk = " ".join(words[start:end]).strip()

        if chunk:
            chunks.append(chunk)

        if end >= len(words):
            break

    return chunks

def build_chunks(pages: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    """Convert OCR pages into indexable chunks."""

    chunks: list[dict] = []

    chunk_id = 0

    for page in pages:
        text = page["text"]

        page_chunks = chunk_text(
            text,
            chunk_tokens=CHUNK_TOKENS,
            overlap=OVERLAP,
        )

        for local_chunk_id, chunk_text_value in enumerate(page_chunks):

            chunks.append(
                {
                    "id": f"chunk-{chunk_id:06d}",
                    "page_id": page.get("page_id"),
                    "page_number": page.get("page_number"),
                    "chapter": page.get("chapter"),
                    "doc_id": page.get("doc_id", "default"),
                    "source_image": page.get("source_image"),
                    "text": chunk_text_value,
                    "chunk_index": local_chunk_id,
                }
            )

            chunk_id += 1

    return chunks

# ============================================================
# Device
# ============================================================

def get_device() -> str:
    """Use CUDA when available, otherwise CPU."""

    try:
        import torch

        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            print("Using CUDA")
            print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0))
            return "cuda"

    except Exception as exc:
        print("CUDA check failed:", exc)

    print("Using CPU")

    return "cpu"

# ============================================================
# Embeddings
# ============================================================

def build_embeddings(
    chunks: list[dict],
    device: str,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Generate normalized BGE-M3 embeddings."""

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("Loading embedding model")
    print("=" * 60)
    print(MODEL_NAME)

    model = SentenceTransformer(
        MODEL_NAME,
        device=device,
    )

    actual_dim = model.get_sentence_embedding_dimension()

    if actual_dim != EMBED_DIM:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Expected embedding dimension {EMBED_DIM}, "
            f"but model produces {actual_dim}."
        )

    texts = [chunk["text"] for chunk in chunks]

    print()
    print(f"Embedding {len(texts)} chunks...")
    print(f"Batch size: {BATCH_SIZE}")

    vectors = model.encode(
        texts,
        batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
        show_progress_bar=True,
        convert_to_numpy=True,
        normalize_embeddings=True,
    )

    vectors = np.asarray(
        vectors,
        dtype=np.float32,
    )

    if vectors.shape != (len(chunks), EMBED_DIM):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unexpected embedding shape: {vectors.shape}; "
            f"expected {(len(chunks), EMBED_DIM)}"
        )

    return vectors

# ============================================================
# FAISS
# ============================================================

def build_faiss_index(vectors: np.ndarray):
    """Build an inner-product FAISS HNSW index."""

    dimension = vectors.shape[1]

    # Inner product + normalized vectors = cosine similarity.
    index = faiss.IndexHNSWFlat(
        dimension,
        32,
        faiss.METRIC_INNER_PRODUCT,
    )

    index.hnsw.efConstruction = 200

    index.add(vectors)

    return index

# ============================================================
# Save
# ============================================================

def save_index(
    index,
    chunks: list[dict],
    vectors: np.ndarray,
):
    """Save FAISS index, metadata, and embeddings."""

    INDEX_DIR.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    index_path = INDEX_DIR / "index.faiss"
    chunks_path = INDEX_DIR / "chunks.json"
    vectors_path = INDEX_DIR / "vectors.npy"

    faiss.write_index(
        index,
        str(index_path),
    )

    with chunks_path.open(
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8",
    ) as f:
        json.dump(
            chunks,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        )

    np.save(
        vectors_path,
        vectors,
    )

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("INDEX BUILT SUCCESSFULLY")
    print("=" * 60)

    print(f"Chunks:   {len(chunks)}")
    print(f"Vectors:  {vectors.shape}")
    print(f"FAISS:    {index_path}")
    print(f"Metadata: {chunks_path}")
    print(f"Vectors:  {vectors_path}")

# ============================================================
# Main
# ============================================================

def main():
    print("=" * 60)
    print("BUILD INDEX FROM EXISTING OCR")
    print("=" * 60)

    print()
    print("OCR file:")
    print(OCR_FILE)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load OCR
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    pages = load_ocr_pages(OCR_FILE)

    print()
    print(f"Loaded {len(pages)} OCR pages")

    if not pages:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No OCR pages were found."
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Chunk
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    chunks = build_chunks(pages)

    print()
    print(f"Created {len(chunks)} chunks")

    if not chunks:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No chunks were created from OCR text."
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Device
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    device = get_device()

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Embeddings
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    vectors = build_embeddings(
        chunks,
        device,
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. FAISS
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("Building FAISS HNSW index")
    print("=" * 60)

    index = build_faiss_index(vectors)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Save
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    save_index(
        index,
        chunks,
        vectors,
    )

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Before running

Your OCR file needs to actually be at:

data/interim/ocr_pages.jsonl

Check:

ls -lh data/interim/ocr_pages.jsonl

If it's somewhere else, change:

OCR_FILE = Path("data/interim/ocr_pages.jsonl")

at the top.

Install FAISS if necessary

Check:

python -c "import faiss; print(faiss.__version__)"

If that fails:

uv pip install faiss-cpu

You don't need a CUDA version of FAISS for this index.

Run it

Because you're using the project's virtual environment:

python scripts/build_index_from_ocr.py

You do not need:

PYTHONPATH="$PWD/src" bash scripts/build_index.sh

and you do not need to run run_ingest.py.

This standalone script completely bypasses the normal ingestion pipeline.

One important point about your existing project

Your original configuration says:

index:
  type: "faiss:hnsw"
  path: "data/index"
  chunk_tokens: 256
  overlap: 32

The script above intentionally uses those same:

chunk_tokens = 256
overlap = 32

and creates:

data/index/
├── index.faiss
├── chunks.json
└── vectors.npy

The metadata preserves the information from your OCR JSONL:

{
  "page_id": "...",
  "page_number": 14,
  "chapter": 1,
  "source_image": "...",
  "text": "..."
}

So later retrieval can still tell your RAG system which chapter/page produced the retrieved chunk.

One caveat

This standalone script uses whitespace-based chunking, while your project's actual chunk.split() implementation may use a different tokenization strategy. If your A2 specification requires the index to be produced exactly through the project's existing Stage 4 chunker, I would instead make a small build_index_from_ocr.py that feeds these OCR records into your existing chunk.split() and embed.encode() functions. That would be preferable for consistency with the rest of your A2 code.

If you show me your src/doc_agent/chunk.py and src/doc_agent/index/store.py, I can give you the project-native version that creates exactly the same index format your existing retriever expects.
